   939	      private function qolGameTick() : void
   940	      {
   941	         ++this.qolPerfFrame;
   942	         if(this.§_-BF§ == LEVEL_NORMAL)
   943	         {
   944	            this.§_-HR§();
   945	         }
   946	         if(this.§_-BF§ == LEVEL_NORMAL || this.§_-BF§ == LEVEL_PRE_WIN)
   947	         {
   948	            var heavy:Boolean = this.entities.numChildren > 180 || this.bullets.numChildren > 220;
   949	            var extreme:Boolean = this.entities.numChildren > 300 || this.bullets.numChildren > 380;
   950	            this.updateEntities();
   951	            this.updateBullets();
   952	            if(!heavy || !extreme && (this.qolPerfFrame & 1) == 0 || extreme && this.qolPerfFrame % 4 == 0)
   953	            {
   954	               this.updateEnemyDecals();
   955	               this.updateBulletsDecals();
   956	               this.updateDecals();
   957	               this.updateBackground();
   958	            }
   959	            this.§_-ai§();
   960	            this.menu.updateMenu();
   961	            this.§include return§.update();
   962	            this.updatePointers();
   963	         }
   964	      }

  1115	      private function qolSettingsClick(param1:MouseEvent) : void
  1116	      {
  1117	         var action:String = Sprite(param1.currentTarget).name;
  1118	         if(action == "speed")
  1119	         {
  1120	            Level.qolSpeed = Level.qolSpeed == 1 ? 3 : 1;
  1121	         }
  1122	         else if(action == "gold_add")
  1123	         {
  1124	            var amount:Number = this.qolGoldInput == null ? 0 : Number(this.qolGoldInput.text);
  1125	            if(isNaN(amount) || amount < 0)
  1126	            {
  1127	               amount = 0;
  1128	            }
  1129	            this.updateCash(int(Math.min(2000000000,amount)));
  1130	         }
  1131	         else if(action == "trees_toggle")
  1132	         {
  1133	            this.game.qolSetTreesMaxed(!this.game.qolTreesMaxed);
  1134	         }
  1135	         else if(action == "page_heroes")
  1136	         {
  1137	            this.qolSettingsPage = 2;
  1138	         }
  1139	         else if(action.indexOf("hero_") == 0)
  1140	         {
  1141	            this.qolToggleHero(action.substr(5));
  1142	         }
  1143	         else if(action == "all_waves")
  1144	         {
  1145	            this.qolSendAllWaves();
  1146	         }
  1147	         else if(action == "page_enemy")
  1148	         {
  1149	            this.qolSettingsPage = 1;
  1150	         }
  1151	         else if(action == "page_main")
  1152	         {
  1153	            this.qolSettingsPage = 0;
  1154	         }
  1155	         else if(action == "enemy_prev")
  1156	         {
  1157	            this.qolEnemyIndex = (this.qolEnemyIndex + this.qolEnemies.length - 1) % this.qolEnemies.length;
  1158	         }
  1159	         else if(action == "enemy_next")
  1160	         {
  1161	            this.qolEnemyIndex = (this.qolEnemyIndex + 1) % this.qolEnemies.length;
  1162	         }
  1163	         else if(action == "count_minus")
  1164	         {
  1165	            this.qolEnemyCount = Math.max(1,this.qolEnemyCount - 5);
  1166	         }
  1167	         else if(action == "count_plus")
  1168	         {
  1169	            this.qolEnemyCount = Math.min(200,this.qolEnemyCount + 5);
  1170	         }
  1171	         else if(action == "path_prev")
  1172	         {
  1173	            this.qolEnemyPath = Math.max(0,this.qolEnemyPath - 1);
  1174	         }
  1175	         else if(action == "path_next")
  1176	         {
  1177	            this.qolEnemyPath = Math.min(Math.max(0,this.§_-V8§.length - 1),this.qolEnemyPath + 1);
  1178	         }
  1179	         else if(action == "send_custom")
  1180	         {
  1181	            this.qolSendCustomRound();
  1182	         }
  1183	         else if(action == "hide")
  1184	         {
  1185	            this.qolHideSettings();
  1186	            return;
  1187	         }
  1188	         this.qolRenderSettings();
  1189	      }
  1190	      
  1191	      private function qolSendCustomRound() : void

   939	      private function qolGameTick() : void
   940	      {
   941	         ++this.qolPerfFrame;
   942	         if(this.§_-BF§ == LEVEL_NORMAL)
   943	         {
   944	            this.§_-HR§();
   945	         }
   946	         if(this.§_-BF§ == LEVEL_NORMAL || this.§_-BF§ == LEVEL_PRE_WIN)
   947	         {
   948	            var heavy:Boolean = this.entities.numChildren > 180 || this.bullets.numChildren > 220;
   949	            var extreme:Boolean = this.entities.numChildren > 300 || this.bullets.numChildren > 380;
   950	            this.updateEntities();
   951	            this.updateBullets();
   952	            if(!heavy || !extreme && (this.qolPerfFrame & 1) == 0 || extreme && this.qolPerfFrame % 4 == 0)
   953	            {
   954	               this.updateEnemyDecals();
   955	               this.updateBulletsDecals();
   956	               this.updateDecals();
   957	               this.updateBackground();
   958	            }
   959	            this.§_-ai§();
   960	            this.menu.updateMenu();
   961	            this.§include return§.update();
   962	            this.updatePointers();
   963	         }
   964	      }

  1050	      private function qolRenderSettings() : void
  1051	      {
  1052	         if(this.qolSettings == null)
  1053	         {
  1054	            return;
  1055	         }
  1056	         while(this.qolSettings.numChildren > 0)
  1057	         {
  1058	            this.qolSettings.removeChildAt(0);
  1059	         }
  1060	         this.qolSettings.graphics.clear();
  1061	         this.qolSettings.graphics.beginFill(1118481,0.97);
  1062	         this.qolSettings.graphics.lineStyle(2,13983051,0.8);
  1063	         this.qolSettings.graphics.drawRoundRect(0,0,580,455,18,18);
  1064	         this.qolSettings.graphics.endFill();
  1065	         if(this.qolSettingsPage == 0)
  1066	         {
  1067	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolLabel("V4 LEVEL SETTINGS",28,20,24));
  1068	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolButton("Speed: " + (Level.qolSpeed == 3 ? "3x" : "1x"),28,72,250,"speed"));
  1069	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolButton(this.game.qolTreesMaxed ? "RESET FOR CUSTOM" : "MAX ALL",302,72,250,"trees_toggle"));
  1070	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolLabel("Add gold:",28,142,18));
  1071	            this.qolGoldInput = this.qolInput("0",130,130,215);
  1072	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolGoldInput);
  1073	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolButton("ADD",360,130,192,"gold_add"));
  1074	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolButton("Hero selection  →",28,202,250,"page_heroes"));
  1075	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolButton(this.qolSendAllPending ? "Sending all waves…" : "Send all waves",302,202,250,"all_waves"));
  1076	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolButton("Enemy tools  →",28,262,250,"page_enemy"));
  1077	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolButton("Hide V4 tools",302,262,250,"hide"));
  1078	            this.qolSettings.addChild(this.qolLabel("Next-wave flags now appear immediately after the previous wave is sent.",28,330,14));

  1115	      private function qolSettingsClick(param1:MouseEvent) : void
  1116	      {
  1117	         var action:String = Sprite(param1.currentTarget).name;
  1118	         if(action == "speed")
  1119	         {
  1120	            Level.qolSpeed = Level.qolSpeed == 1 ? 3 : 1;
  1121	         }
  1122	         else if(action == "gold_add")
  1123	         {
  1124	            var amount:Number = this.qolGoldInput == null ? 0 : Number(this.qolGoldInput.text);
  1125	            if(isNaN(amount) || amount < 0)
  1126	            {
  1127	               amount = 0;
  1128	            }
  1129	            this.updateCash(int(Math.min(2000000000,amount)));
  1130	         }
  1131	         else if(action == "trees_toggle")
  1132	         {
  1133	            this.game.qolSetTreesMaxed(!this.game.qolTreesMaxed);
  1134	         }
  1135	         else if(action == "page_heroes")
  1136	         {
  1137	            this.qolSettingsPage = 2;
  1138	         }
  1139	         else if(action.indexOf("hero_") == 0)
  1140	         {
  1141	            this.qolToggleHero(action.substr(5));
  1142	         }
  1143	         else if(action == "all_waves")
  1144	         {
  1145	            this.qolSendAllWaves();
  1146	         }
  1147	         else if(action == "page_enemy")
  1148	         {
  1149	            this.qolSettingsPage = 1;
  1150	         }
  1151	         else if(action == "page_main")
  1152	         {
  1153	            this.qolSettingsPage = 0;
  1154	         }
  1155	         else if(action == "enemy_prev")
  1156	         {
  1157	            this.qolEnemyIndex = (this.qolEnemyIndex + this.qolEnemies.length - 1) % this.qolEnemies.length;
  1158	         }
  1159	         else if(action == "enemy_next")
  1160	         {
  1161	            this.qolEnemyIndex = (this.qolEnemyIndex + 1) % this.qolEnemies.length;
  1162	         }
  1163	         else if(action == "count_minus")
  1164	         {
  1165	            this.qolEnemyCount = Math.max(1,this.qolEnemyCount - 5);
  1166	         }
  1167	         else if(action == "count_plus")
  1168	         {
  1169	            this.qolEnemyCount = Math.min(200,this.qolEnemyCount + 5);
  1170	         }
  1171	         else if(action == "path_prev")
  1172	         {
  1173	            this.qolEnemyPath = Math.max(0,this.qolEnemyPath - 1);
  1174	         }
  1175	         else if(action == "path_next")
  1176	         {
  1177	            this.qolEnemyPath = Math.min(Math.max(0,this.§_-V8§.length - 1),this.qolEnemyPath + 1);
  1178	         }
  1179	         else if(action == "send_custom")
  1180	         {
  1181	            this.qolSendCustomRound();
  1182	         }
  1183	         else if(action == "hide")
  1184	         {
  1185	            this.qolHideSettings();
  1186	            return;
  1187	         }
  1188	         this.qolRenderSettings();
  1189	      }
